I infer an empty-stabilize-direct sequence and treat desire as the vessel's new orientation; a live alternative is the ordinary temporal sequence of finishing one task and beginning another with no container transfer.","changed_reading":{"before":"When free, undertake another labor and afterward turn devotionally to Rabb.","after":"Completion empties a vessel of attention, exertion sets it upright, and «فارغب» directs the newly open capacity toward Rabb before vacancy can scatter it."}},{"model_id":"c07_aspiration_runs_through_recurrent_labor","status":"strengthened","confidence":"medium","trigger_roots":["ف ر غ","ن ص ب"],"mechanism":"Vacancy after one occupation, renewed intention, and exhausting labor make a recurrent work-cycle. The Rabb branch of staying and the desire branch of direction move aspiration inside that cycle as its durable axis rather than reserving it for leisure after work.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"94:7","root":"ف ر غ","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001147","branch_id":"B001","role":"The literal vacancy after occupation marks a transition point rather than permanent release from action."},{"source_ref":"94:7","root":"ف ر غ","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001147","branch_id":"B006","role":"The literal intention toward a matter turns the vacant interval into renewed purposive engagement."},{"source_ref":"94:7","root":"ن ص ب","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001507","branch_id":"B004","role":"The literal exhausting toil keeps the renewed engagement materially strenuous rather than merely contemplative."},{"source_ref":"94:8","root":"ر ب ب","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000532","branch_id":"B007","role":"The literal image of staying, clinging, lasting, and drawing near supplies continuity across successive episodes of work."},{"source_ref":"94:8","root":"ر غ ب","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000575","branch_id":"B001","role":"The literal directed inclination supplies the stable orientation carried through vacancy, intention, and toil."}],"structural_cues":["The two final imperatives are adjacent rather than separated into a work section and a devotional appendix.","The conditional transition in 94:7 is followed immediately by the explicit destination in 94:8."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies post-occupation vacancy, fresh intention, toil, durable staying, and directed desire. I infer that orientation persists through the recurrent labor-cycle; a live alternative is a strictly sequential reading in which aspiration begins only after the new exertion is complete.","changed_reading":{"before":"Complete worldly work, perform a separate devotional labor, and then long for Rabb.","after":"Carry longing toward Rabb as the continuous axis through each cycle of completion, renewed intention, and strenuous work."}},{"model_id":"c08_received_acts_become_answering_desire","status":"revised","confidence":"strong","trigger_roots":["ش ر ح","و ض ع","ر ف ع"],"mechanism":"Opening, putting down the burden, and raising the addressee's mention form a pattern of prior action upon the addressee. Read through Rabb as nurture and completion, the final imperative becomes reciprocal: desire answers a formative agency already experienced across the window.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"94:1","root":"ش ر ح","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000784","branch_id":"B001","role":"The literal opening and clarification supplies the first formative act performed for the addressee."},{"source_ref":"94:2","root":"و ض ع","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001657","branch_id":"B001","role":"The literal putting down into a lower or settled place supplies the formative act of unloading what had pressed on the addressee."},{"source_ref":"94:4","root":"ر ف ع","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000582","branch_id":"B001","role":"The literal raising of a thing supplies the complementary upward act that restores and elevates the addressee."},{"source_ref":"94:8","root":"ر ب ب","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000532","branch_id":"B002","role":"The literal image of repair, administration, nurture, and completion gathers the prior opening, unloading, and raising into a coherent Rabb-like agency."},{"source_ref":"94:8","root":"ر غ ب","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000575","branch_id":"B001","role":"The literal turn of desire toward something supplies the answering movement from the formed addressee back toward the formative Rabb."}],"structural_cues":["The earlier first-person-plural acts are done for, away from, or in favor of the same second-person addressee who receives the final imperatives.","The last ayah names «your Rabb» only after the window has displayed a sequence of opening, unloading, and raising."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies the ordered acts of opening, putting down, and raising plus the Rabb branch of staged nurture. I infer that «your Rabb» retrospectively profiles the agent of those acts and that desire is a reciprocal answer; a live alternative is that the acts remain separate favors and Rabb functions only as the command's conventional title.","changed_reading":{"before":"The final ayah issues a standalone demand that the addressee desire the Lord.","after":"The addressee's desire is an answering movement toward the Rabb already encountered as opener, unloader, raiser, and gradual completer."}}],"surprising_valid_outliers":[{"outlier_id":"o_split_rabb_as_rising_breath","confidence":"exploratory","containment":"This is surprising because it uses the packet's non-dominant ر ب و mapping for the focus root and links it to chest-opening and exertion. It remains anchored in an explicitly supplied mapped focus branch and the final directional construction, but downstream prose should present it only as an embodied resonance, never as a replacement gloss or etymology for Rabb.","focus_anchor":"The packet preserves root_000537 for the focus occurrence of ر ب ب, while «إلى» and the desire imperative retain the focus's directional control.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"94:1","root":"ص د ر","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000849","branch_id":"B001","role":"The literal bodily chest supplies the organ through which an imagistic breath of aspiration can be enacted."},{"source_ref":"94:7","root":"ن ص ب","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001507","branch_id":"B004","role":"The literal exhausting toil supplies the bodily condition that produces labored or rising breath."},{"source_ref":"94:8","root":"ر ب ب","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000537","branch_id":"B004","role":"The literal image of panting or breath swelling upward supplies an embodied rising motion at the focus endpoint."},{"source_ref":"94:8","root":"ر غ ب","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000575","branch_id":"B001","role":"The literal directed inclination converts rising breath from a bodily symptom into a contained image of aspiration toward Rabb."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"After exertion, direct a mental or devotional desire toward Rabb.","after":"The opened, laboring body releases a rising breath of desire toward Rabb, so aspiration is somatically enacted as well as inwardly intended."}},{"outlier_id":"o_erected_object_corrected_by_endpoint","confidence":"exploratory","containment":"This is surprising because the cult-stone branch of ن ص ب is remote from the immediate imperative sense. It remains valid as a contrast activated by the directly preceding root and checked by the focus's explicit endpoint; downstream prose should render it as a prophylactic ritual resonance, not as a translation of «فانصب» or an allegation of actual object-worship.","focus_anchor":"The focus explicitly directs desire to Rabb, whose dominant branch supplies authority and ownership.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"94:7","root":"ن ص ب","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001507","branch_id":"B002","role":"The literal image of an erected stone for worship or slaughter raises the possibility that an installed ritual means could capture attention."},{"source_ref":"94:8","root":"ر غ ب","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000575","branch_id":"B001","role":"The literal toward-or-away vector of desire lets the final endpoint correct any fixation on the erected form."},{"source_ref":"94:8","root":"ر ب ب","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000532","branch_id":"B001","role":"The literal image of lordship and ownership identifies the authority beyond the installed ritual object at which desire must terminate."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"Set yourself to worship and then desire your Lord.","after":"Whatever form is erected or enacted in worship, desire must pass beyond the installed means and terminate at Rabb; the endpoint guards ritual orientation."}},{"outlier_id":"o_covenantal_return_of_bestowed_name","confidence":"exploratory","containment":"This is surprising because it joins distant covenant and documentary branches to the raised mention. It remains anchored in a focus branch of ر ب ب, the focus desire vector, and the explicit social action in 94:4; downstream prose should qualify it as a juridical-social resonance rather than a lexical gloss of Rabb or ذكر.","focus_anchor":"A covenant branch belongs to the dominant mapped inventory of the focus root ر ب ب, and directed desire supplies the addressee's responsive act.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"94:4","root":"ر ف ع","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000582","branch_id":"B001","role":"The literal raising of a thing supplies the bestowed elevation whose use now calls for an answering relation."},{"source_ref":"94:4","root":"ذ ك ر","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000516","branch_id":"B008","role":"The literal deed or document of a right recasts elevated mention as something entrusted and answerable rather than merely enjoyed."},{"source_ref":"94:8","root":"ر ب ب","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000532","branch_id":"B011","role":"The literal covenant, pact, or protection agreement supplies a binding relational frame for «your Rabb»."},{"source_ref":"94:8","root":"ر غ ب","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000575","branch_id":"B001","role":"The literal turn of inclination supplies the addressee's active return-performance within the covenantal frame."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"Respond to raised reputation by privately desiring Rabb.","after":"The raised name behaves like an entrusted deed within a pact, and directed desire becomes the addressee's return of fidelity rather than consumption of status."}},{"outlier_id":"o_ravenous_appetite_transmuted","confidence":"exploratory","containment":"This is surprising because it carries the greed and gluttony branch of ر غ ب into a devotional imperative. It remains directly anchored in the focus root and is contained by Rabb's authority plus the toward-or-away branch; downstream prose should use it only as an analogy for intensity and redirection, never as praise of greed or excess.","focus_anchor":"Both the ravenous-appetite branch and the directional branch belong to the focus occurrence of ر غ ب, and Rabb supplies the governing endpoint.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"94:8","root":"ر غ ب","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000575","branch_id":"B003","role":"The literal image of ravenous appetite or greed supplies an unsettling intensity and near-insatiability to the act of wanting."},{"source_ref":"94:8","root":"ر غ ب","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000575","branch_id":"B001","role":"The literal capacity of desire to turn toward or away converts raw appetite into a redirectable force rather than endorsing its former objects."},{"source_ref":"94:8","root":"ر ب ب","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000532","branch_id":"B001","role":"The literal authority and ownership of Rabb disciplines the appetite's object and keeps intensity under governance."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"Approach Rabb with restrained, decorous preference.","after":"Do not merely suppress appetite; transfer its full hunger toward Rabb, where its intensity is governed and its former objects are displaced."}}],"summary":{"strongest_changes":["The opened chest became an enlarged source of action whose downstream expression is directed desire.","The removed burden and emptied post-work vessel became released capacity, so the final imperative uses relief rather than adding a new weight.","Repeated hardship and ease became the medium of orientation; neither escape from one nor possession of the other remains desire's endpoint.","Raised reputation and the prior sequence of opening, unloading, and raising turned desire into a reciprocal return toward the formative Rabb."],"what_was_special":"The focus sustained several coexisting mechanisms: sovereign redirection, consent to nurture, spatial capacity, giver-over-gift, anti-reputational reversal, an empty-and-upright vessel sequence, and aspiration continuous with labor. Form-distant but packet-backed activations added rising breath, ritual endpoint correction, covenantal fidelity, and transmuted appetite without being promoted to exclusive readings.","remaining_uncertainties":["The causal arrows from chest-opening, unloading, and post-work vacancy into desire are reader-supplied; the context may instead present adjacent favors and commands.","The toward construction strongly fixes a positive endpoint, but its implication of turning away from every rival endpoint remains an inference.","The non-dominant ر ب و breath resonance and the cult-stone and covenantal models are imagistic activations, not replacement lexical senses.","Capacious longing for bounty and longing that passes beyond bounty to the giver remain live together rather than fully resolving into one model."]}}}},"v12_reader_walks":{"reader_a":{"activated_readings_md":"1. Desire is finally given direction. Lexical evidence: `ر غ ب B001` desire turning toward or away; `ر ب ب B001` lordship/ownership/authority and `ر ب ب B002` repair, nurture, completion. Structural cue: the prepositional phrase `إِلَىٰ رَبِّكَ` precedes the imperative, making the destination explicit. Assigned role: `رب` is the proper object of the capacity opened in 94:1 and emptied in 94:7. Abductive move supplied by me: the passage assumes desire must be governed, not merely intensified. Reading change: the ayah reads as orientation of the newly freed self toward its completing caretaker.\n\n2. The final desire reverses worldly expansion. Lexical evidence: earlier `ش ر ح B005` expansive inclination toward worldly things; `ر غ ب B001` includes desire toward and aversion/turning away. Structural cue: 94:1 opens the chest; 94:8 directs رغبة to the Lord. Assigned role: the same inner expansion that could spread toward possession is narrowed and purified by direction. Abductive move supplied by me: `إلى ربك` both attracts desire toward the Lord and implies turning away from rival objects. Reading change: the ayah becomes disciplined desire, not generic longing.\n\n3. Lordship is read as nurturing completion of the whole bodily process. Lexical evidence: `ر ب ب B002` repair, nurture, completion stage by stage; `ر ح م B001/B003` mercy and womb; `و ض ع B002` birth; `ع س ر B006` difficult labor; `ي س ر B006` productive increase. Structural cue: the surah begins with mercy, moves through chest/burden/back, repeats hardship/ease, and ends at `ربك`. Assigned role: the Lord is the one who tends the process from constriction to delivery to growth. Abductive move supplied by me: the final `رب` gathers the earlier maternal/generative branches into a theology of nurture. Reading change: the ayah reads as return to the caretaker of growth, not only submission to authority.\n\n4. Spacious desire answers spacious ease. Lexical evidence: `ر غ ب B002` physical breadth/capaciousness; `ي س ر B001` ease/readiness; `ف ر غ B003` breadth in movement/road; `ص د ر B001/B002` chest/front opening. Structural cue: spatial language of opening, ease, road-breadth, and capacious desire accumulates across the packet. Assigned role: desire becomes the widened interior’s directed motion. Abductive move supplied by me: the surah’s “ease” is not just lower pressure but more navigable space for longing. Reading change: `فارغب` reads as spacious movement toward the Lord.\n\n5. A gift/bounty and covenant reading is preserved. Lexical evidence: `ر غ ب B004` abundant desirable gift; `ر ب ب B011` covenant or pact, `ر ب ب B016` need/firm knot/favor; `ي س ر B003` prosperity/means; `ذ ك ر B008` document of right. Structural cue: earlier burden/liability and raised mention are followed by final directed desire to the Lord. Assigned role: final رغبة can be desire for bounty within a binding relation, not appetite without limit. Abductive move supplied by me: the one who removes burden and raises mention is also the grantor with whom covenantal dependence is renewed. Reading change: the ayah can read as covenantal seeking of favor, not mere psychological yearning.","retrospective_surprises_md":"No later ayah follows 94:8, so the retrospective surprise comes from how the whole previous sequence accumulates into it. The final `فارغب` gathers the opened chest, removed burden, raised mention, repeated ease, and renewed exertion into one directed motion. The biggest rereading is that desire is not an added devotional ending; it is the final governance of every earlier expansion. The `ر ب ب B002` nurture branch also retroactively seals the mercy/womb thread opened in 94:0.","turkish_prose_synthesis_md":null,"source_file":"quran-data/data/analysis/ayah-activation/v12-tr/s094/full_context_control/reader_s094_a_ayah_walk.md"}},"v12_reader_walks_wide":{"reader_a":{"activated_readings_md":"1. **Desire disciplined into a single vector of allegiance.** Fixed `ر غ ب/B001` includes desire turning toward or away, and fixed `ر ب ب/B001` supplies lordship, ownership, and obeyed authority. The preposition **إلى** selects the toward-pole of رغبة; 94:7 has just cleared attention and established an upright stance. The desire branch supplies directionality, while the lordship branch supplies the authoritative destination. **Abductive move:** I infer cleared attention -> exclusive orientation -> obedient action. **Reading change:** **فارغب** is not free-floating wish but a directed allegiance whose object and heading are grammatically fixed.\n\n2. **Desire for the one who completes formation stage by stage.** Fixed `ر ب ب/B002` is repair, nurture, administration, and completion over successive stages; fixed `ر غ ب/B001` supplies inclination toward that agent. The sequence back-strain -> raised mention -> repeated opening -> fresh exertion in 94:3-7 has the shape of iterative formation rather than one terminal rescue. The Lord branch supplies the administrator of stages; desire supplies willing approach. **Abductive move:** I infer that the preceding changes are stages administered by the named Lord and that the final command seeks continued formation. **Reading change:** **ربك** becomes specifically the nurturer-completer of the process, and desire becomes consent to its next stage.\n\n3. **An approach that widens as it advances.** Fixed `ر غ ب/B002` is breadth, capaciousness, or expansive taking of ground; fixed `ر ب ب/B007` includes staying, lasting, and drawing near. In 94:5-6, ease restores readiness; in 94:7, `ف ر غ/B003` opens breadth of gait and sustained movement. The رغبة branch supplies expanding capacity or stride, while the رب branch supplies durable nearness. **Abductive move:** I infer that the final directed desire spatializes the recovered capacity as a widening, sustained approach. **Reading change:** the closing imperative can be heard not only as wanting but as enlarging one's range toward lasting nearness.\n\n4. **The giver, not merely the desirable gift, becomes the object.** Fixed `ر غ ب/B004` is an abundant desirable bounty, while fixed `ر ب ب/B016` includes need and benefaction. Raised mention and repeated ease in 94:4-6 can occupy the role of received benefits, yet the fixed syntax directs رغبة to **ربك**. **Abductive move:** I infer that experienced benefits train desire to pass from bounty to its administering benefactor; the packet supplies benefits and destination, not the pedagogical arrow. **Reading change:** the final orientation gathers prior gifts without making them the terminus of desire.","retrospective_surprises_md":"No later ayah exists in packet order, so no later-ayah surprise emerged. The closing direction remains retrospectively shaped only by earlier ayat in its 94:3-94:8 focus window.","turkish_prose_synthesis_md":null,"source_file":"quran-data/data/analysis/ayah-activation/v12-tr-11ayah/s094/full_context_control/reader_s094_a_ayah_walk.md"}},"v12_cross_run_publication":{"source_file":"quran-data/data/analysis/ayah-activation/v12-cross-run/tr/94_ayah_findings_publication.json","protocol":"v12-cross-run-publication-v3","language":"tr","surah":94,"ayah_ref":"94:8","canonical_ayah_ref":"94:8","baseline":{"source_ref":"94:8","text":"Ve Rabbine yönel.","target_tokens":[["Ve",["94:8:1"]],["Rabbine",["94:8:1","94:8:2"]],["yönel",["94:8:3"]]]},"findings":[{"text":"Önce açılan, yükten kurtarılan ve yeniden doğrultulan kapasite, burada tek bir itaat yönüne bağlanır; istek serbestçe dolaşan bir arzu değil, Rabbine dönük bağlılıktır.","grade":"strong","anchors":[["94:8:2","root_000532",["B001"]],["94:8:3","root_000575",["B001"]]]},{"text":"Rab, önceki rahatlatma ve yeniden çalıştırma aşamalarını adım adım onarıp olgunlaştıran yetiştirici olarak belirir; yöneliş de bu oluşumun sonraki aşamasına gönüllü katılımdır.","grade":"strong","anchors":[["94:8:2","root_000532",["B002"]],["94:8:3","root_000575",["B001"]]]},{"text":"İlk ayetteki dünyaya doğru yayılabilen iç genişleme ihtimali, burada isteğin Rabbine çevrilmesiyle sınırlandırılıp arındırılır.","grade":"weak","anchors":[["94:8:3","root_000575",["B001"]]]},{"text":"Genişlik ve kalıcı yakınlık çağrışımları, son yönelişi yalnızca istemek değil, Rabbine yaklaştıkça hareket alanı genişleyen sürekli bir ilerleyiş olarak gösterir.","grade":"weak","anchors":[["94:8:2","root_000532",["B007"]],["94:8:3","root_000575",["B002"]]]},{"text":"Önceki rahatlama ve yükseltilme nimetleri arzunun son hedefi olmaz; istek, armağanlardan onları veren ve ihtiyacı karşılayan Rabbe geçer.","grade":"weak","anchors":[["94:8:2","root_000532",["B016"]],["94:8:3","root_000575",["B004"]]]},{"text":"Armağan ve bağlayıcı söz çağrışımları, son yönelişi sınırsız bir iştah değil, Rab ile kurulan bağlılık içinde iyilik arayışı olarak da düşündürebilir.","grade":"reject","anchors":[["94:8:2","root_000532",["B011","B016"]],["94:8:3","root_000575",["B004"]]]}]},"butuncul_okuma_line":{"raw_line":"**94:8.** وَإِلَىٰ رَبِّكَ فَٱرْغَب — Birincil okuma: Son ayet, açılan, yükten kurtulan ve yeniden işe dikilen benliğin arzusunu yalnız Rabbine yöneltir. Böylece rağbet sıradan istek değil, terbiye eden, onaran ve tamamlayan Rabbe doğru genişleyen bir hareket olur; önceki rahmet, doğum, kolaylık, zikir ve emek çizgileri burada disiplinli bir bağlılıkta birleşir. {ر غ ب B001; ر غ ب B002; ر غ ب B004; ر ب ب B001; ر ب ب B002; ر ب ب B011; ر ح م B001; ش ر ح B005; ي س ر B001}","arabic_text":"وَإِلَىٰ رَبِّكَ فَٱرْغَب","reading_text_tr":"Son ayet, açılan, yükten kurtulan ve yeniden işe dikilen benliğin arzusunu yalnız Rabbine yöneltir. Böylece rağbet sıradan istek değil, terbiye eden, onaran ve tamamlayan Rabbe doğru genişleyen bir hareket olur; önceki rahmet, doğum, kolaylık, zikir ve emek çizgileri burada disiplinli bir bağlılıkta birleşir.","root_citations":["ر غ ب B001","ر غ ب B002","ر غ ب B004","ر ب ب B001","ر ب ب B002","ر ب ب B011","ر ح م B001","ش ر ح B005","ي س ر raised mention belongs to the preceding relief-and-restoration sequence."},{"label":"strong","ref":"68:32","note":"Direct desire-to-Lord parallel; shifts attention from a lost object to its desire vocabulary adds a balanced hope-and-fear mode of devoted calling."},{"label":"medium","ref":"94:2","note":"Immediate burden-removal context for the capacity and direction of the closing verse."},{"label":"medium","ref":"9:120","note":"Provides a boundary against preferring oneself away from the committed path."},{"label":"medium","ref":"11:61","note":"Worship, repentance, and a near responsive Lord form a secondary directional pattern."},{"label":"medium","ref":"94:5","note":"Immediate hardship-ease context, though largely repeated by the next ayah."},{"label":"medium","ref":"18:27","note":"V12 presents recitation under the Lord's word as durable alignment rather than alternate refuge."},{"label":"medium","ref":"7:73","note":"Exclusive direction."},{"label":"strong","ref":"110:3","note":"Turns completion and public success into praise and seeking forgiveness directed to the Lord."},{"label":"strong","ref":"74:7","note":"Directly magnification to the Lord, but adds a different devotional act."},{"label":"weak","ref":"9:55","note":"Wealth and offspring serve as a limited contrary case to rightly ordered Yayımlananlar isteme, eğilim, iştah ve aynı kökün bağış dalıyla en açıklayıcı sınırları verir; sevgi ve yoğun bağlılık adayları bunları tekrar ettiği, fiziksel genişlik dalı ile kalan adaylar ise çekirdekle yeterli anlam ortaklığı taşımadığı için seçilmedi.","branch_image_ar":"توجه الرغبة إلى الشيء أو انصرافها عنه","what_is_ar":"يدخل فيه رغبت في الشيء أو إليه بمعنى أردته وملت إليه وحرصت عليه، ورغبت عنه بمعنى لم ترده وزهدت فيه وصرفت الرغبة عنه، وما يتصل بذلك من الرغبة والرغب والرغبى والمرغوب فيه أو عنه.","what_is_not_ar":"ليس المراد هنا السعة الحسية في الجوف أو الحوض أو الأرض، ولا العطاء الكثير من حيث كثرته.","source_phrase_ar":"الرغبة في الشيء الإرادة له؛ رغبت عنه إذا لم ترده (maqayis)؛ إليك الرغباء ومنك النعماء وأنا رغيب عنه إذا تركته عمدا (ayn)؛ رغبت في الشيء إذا ملت إليه ورغبت عنه إذا صددت عنه (jamhara)؛ رغبت في الشئ إذا أردته ورغبت عن الشئ إذا لم ترده وزهدت فيه (sihah)؛ رغب فيه وإليه يقتضي الحرص عليه ورغب عنه اقتضى صرف الرغبة عنه والزهد فيه (mufradat)","sources":["MQ","AY","JA","SI","MU"],"source_note":{}},{"branch_ref":"root_000575/B002","identity_judgment":{"status":"qualified","rationale":"Kaynak ifadesi dalın temelini iç hacim, alan veya hareket açıklığı bakımından genişlik olarak doğrular. Write in tr. Write exactly four files at the paths below and modify nothing else. Do not return one combined response. Omit wrapper labels from the prose and evidence files; the file path already supplies the artifact name.

- Prose: `_commentary/outputs/s094/94_8.prose.tr.md`
- Evidence surface: `_commentary/outputs/s094/94_8.evidence.tr.md`
- Findings index: `_commentary/outputs/s094/94_8.index.tr.md`
- Friction: `_commentary/outputs/s094/94_8.friction.tr.md`

1. **The prose** — per the output contract in `COMMENTARY_SPEC.md` §5 and the task document above: continuous, single voice, no provenance markers, no headers named after evidence layers.
2. **The evidence surface** — separate from the prose, addressable per phrase, per `PRINCIPLES.md` §12.
3. **The findings index** — one line per reading carried by the prose, under its bundle ref, with `[inference]` on the writer's own readings. Include each required `surprise:<id>` synthesis row and its `[supports-primary]` or `[shifts-primary]` relation.
4. A section headed exactly `=== PROMPT FRICTION ===` reporting honestly where the specification above was unclear, self-contradictory, underspecified, or impossible to follow, and any point where you had to invent a rule to proceed. Report this section every time, even if the run went cleanly — say so explicitly if you found nothing — and end it with the density audit required by the task document. This is how the specification gets improved.

## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision. Add no bulk without a distinct
reader payoff, but do not compress a significant finding for brevity.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. They do not license recurrence, maturity, or a surah-wide
  channel name.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by related root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, developed through its final reader-visible result
   in proportion to its significance.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

Start each paragraph with reader meaning before grammar. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi
Allah'a verir; bunu fiille değil, sabit bir ad cümlesiyle yapar" over "Bu âyet,
tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik bir hüküm kurar."

Keep the prose anchored in this ayah's own wording without imposing a paragraph
or length quota. Give every significant finding enough space to make its
mechanism and reader payoff clear, even when it arrives through later context;
never compress it merely to keep the commentary short. Preserve every member of
the resonance set, and use a plain closing synthesis only when the ayah benefits
from one.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
confirm in friction that there was no live misconception, replacement risk, or
counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
